This re-verification caught and fixed a tenth queue defect in `#13244` (equalization compared against the notability-derived pair tier instead of the tier actually written, so an explicit caller tier below the band split the pair — 18 mismatches / 55 pairs).       { "edge_key": "Film_TV|welcome to wrexham -[HAS_AWARD]-> Film_TV_Award|imdb_award_v1|film_20|2022|nominee|critics_choice_documentary_award|sparse", "type": "HAS_AWARD", "from_key": "Film_TV|welcome to wrexham", "to_key": "Film_TV_Award|imdb_award_v1|film_20|2022|nominee|critics_choice_documentary_award|sparse", "properties": { "created_at": "2026-07-31T03:29:09.287Z", "description": "Welcome to Wrexham — Two Hollywood stars. One underdog town. A dream that could change everything.
